Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geological variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 223 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 952 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 223 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the geological structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 952. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)
